# 5.0.0 API–CLI–skill parity matrix

Every public method of every resource class wired into `ConfluenceClient` and `JiraClient` (114 resources, 1115 methods), verified on the final release tree: `main` at `11fb436`, with every release PR #371–#394 merged. The method set is unchanged since the first full verification on the 4.0.0→5.0.0 candidate (`12a7562`) and the re-verification on the code of `0db5030` (#387 on `main`). The release record is [v5.0.0.md](v5.0.0.md).

Result key:

- **PASS**: the CLI reaches the method with every required and supported optional input, the wire contract matches the pinned spec, the skill documents the action well enough to build the command, and a test proves CLI → request.
- **GENERATOR_COVERED**: an `*All` async generator. Its base `list` / `search` action exposes the same inputs plus the pagination flags.
- **ALLOWLISTED**: reachable through an equivalent mirror action, or an input that is deliberately not exposed: deprecated and ignored by Atlassian, removed from the spec, server-managed, not an API parameter, blocked by an API constraint (the API cannot move a page or blog post between spaces), or a one-value spec enum the SDK already sends. The note names where the exemption is pinned and checked by a test.
- **CONNECT_ONLY_DEFERRED**: the CLI sends a correct request, but the spec reserves the operation for Connect apps, which need a per-request Connect JWT. Use the SDK with `createConnectJwtMiddleware`. CLI support is deferred (BACKLOG B1068); the list is pinned as `CONNECT_ONLY` in `test/cli/release-410-parity-analyzers.test.ts`.

| Result                | Methods  |
| --------------------- | -------- |
| PASS                  | 951      |
| GENERATOR_COVERED     | 123      |
| ALLOWLISTED           | 18       |
| CONNECT_ONLY_DEFERRED | 23       |
| GAP                   | 0        |
| **Total**             | **1115** |

Automated guards on every PR: `test/cli/api-skill-parity.test.ts` (no orphan CLI action or skill command, every documented command and action-table row resolves), `test/cli/cli-write-property-reachability.test.ts` (every SDK input, read or write, positional or property, reaches the CLI or is allowlisted with a reason), `test/cli/skill-content.test.ts` (every skill and `--help` example parses, names a real action and carries valid JSON), `test/cli/release-410-parity-analyzers-markers.test.ts` (each of the 170 implemented operations among the 180 that the pinned specs mark x-experimental or deprecated is marked in JSDoc and the skill; the other 10 are deprecated operations the SDK does not implement) and `npm run api-coverage` (route coverage against the pinned specs).
